23:["$","p",null,{"children":"It is running the real thing. There is no recorded trace, no scripted trajectory, no\nhidden ground truth feeding the estimator: what you are watching is a scan matcher\nregistering sweeps against a map it is simultaneously building, a distance transform\nbeing recomputed five times a second, a frontier detector proposing goals, A* routing\nthrough cells that are mostly still unknown, and 56 sampled rollouts being reweighted\ntwenty times a second. Every number in the side panels is read straight out of the\nrunning stack."}]

25:["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The map is worse than the world, and everything downstream inherits that."}]," Turn on\nthe ground-truth overlay and the gray dashed walls will not sit exactly on the dark\ncells. Those beams are ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"withheld from\nmapping"}],", so the map never learns the person and does not need to unlearn them. Same\nmeasurement, opposite treatment, decided by which downstream consumer is asking."]}]
